# CLAIMS ledger (seeded from SPINE at design time; revised after the spine reviews; the editor merges rows)

Key Part I results planned in the spine (2026-11-01; revised the same day after the spine reviews: section numbers follow the
revised Chaps. 4 and 6, Appendix D is renumbered (map in the SPINE change log), rows I-87 onward are new, and IDs of existing rows are
unchanged). "Derived" gives the section (and Appendix D derivation) where the book derives it
step by step; "Cited" gives a public source. **Equation numbers:** only numbers already read from the full text in the planning material
are given; every "eq. to fix" must be read from the source by the writer before the claim enters the text, and the row is then updated.
Status: PLANNED until drafted; WRITTEN (with the \label of the accepted tex where the claim is stated or derived) once its section is
accepted. The derivation checker marks rows CHECKED / WRONG / GAP after drafting. **Merge of the accepted Chapters 1–2 and Appendix A
(2026-11-02):** I-1 to I-27 and I-87 to I-90 are WRITTEN, their claims and sources updated from the SEAM files (equation numbers as read by
the writers); I-110 to I-167 are the new claims those sections derive or cite; I-7, I-13, I-18 to I-20 and I-27 are rewritten without a
literal bar so that the checker reads them; I-48 states the f-sum at any temperature (editor ruling, App. A). **Merge of the accepted Chapter 4 and App. D.6–D.12 (2026-11-02, after the Chapter 4 gate):** I-40 to I-56 and I-93
to I-99 are WRITTEN with the labels of tex/ch04 and tex/appD and the sources the text cites (Bruus and Flensberg without chapter numbers
in 4.7, 4.8 and D.11, editor ruling; the GaAs exciton with the measured ε_∞ = 10.88 of Lockwood et al.); I-171 to I-182 are the new
claims of the SEAM proposals for 4.1–4.9, D-a and D-b. **Merge of the accepted Chapter 3, App. C.1–C.8 and App. D.4–D.5 (2026-11-02):** I-28 to
I-39, I-91 and I-92 are WRITTEN with the labels of tex/ch03 and tex/appD (D.4, D.5), and the sources fixed from the SEAMs (I-33: Rideau
et al. Tables II, VI, VII instead of the unread Yu and Cardona tables; I-36: Aryasetiawan and Gunnarsson Table I, Hybertsen and Louie being
closed access; I-37: Hamann 2013 Eq. (23); I-39: Marzari et al. Eqs. (98), (99)); I-30 is rewritten without a literal bar; I-183 to I-211
are the new claims of the SEAM proposals for 3.1–3.6 and D-a (D.4, D.5). **Merge of the accepted Chapter 5, App. D.13–D.15 and App. C.18
(2026-11-02):** I-57 to I-65 and I-100 to I-102 are WRITTEN with the labels of tex/ch05 and tex/appD (D.13–D.15) as revised after the
Chapter 5 review, and the sources the text cites (I-61: Gonze 1995a, PRA 52, 1086, for the arbitrary-order theorem, beside 1995b, PRA 52,
1096, which the spine cited; I-62: Baroni et al. Eqs. (89)–(91), (98) with the transposed Z* noted; I-63: Mermin credited, the proof is
by trace cyclicity; I-102 is now derived); I-212 to I-234 are the new claims of the SEAM proposals for 5.1–5.7 and D-b (D.13–D.15). I-66
and I-69 (6.1, 6.2, resting on D.13 and D.14) stay PLANNED with their sections. C-18 is WRITTEN. App. C claims are in their own table at
the end (C-1 … C-20). **Merge of the accepted Chapter 6 and App. D.16–D.18 (2026-11-02):** I-66 to I-76 and I-103 to I-106 are WRITTEN
with the labels of tex/ch06 and tex/appD (D.16–D.18) as revised after the Chapter 6 review, and the sources the text cites (I-67: Ibe et al.
Eqs. (1), (2), (55)–(58), Migdal 1941 without equation number; I-69: Verdi and Giustino Eqs. (1), (4); I-73: the three ZPR scales of the
revised 6.3, overscreening "consistent with" Zacharias and Giustino; I-104, I-105: Allen and Eliashberg without equation numbers, Giustino
Eqs. (145), (146), (185), (205), (206)); I-235 to I-260 are the new claims of the SEAM proposals for 6.1–6.5 and D-c (D.16–D.18). **Merge of the accepted Chapter 7 and App. D.19–D.23 (2026-11-02):** I-77 to I-86 and I-107
to I-109 are WRITTEN with the labels of tex/ch07 and tex/appD (D.19–D.23) as revised after the Chapter 7 review, and the sources the text
cites (I-81: Savrasov and Savrasov Table III; I-82: Hochberg et al. Eqs. (2.5)–(2.7) with Λ → e^{−5/6}Λ_D; I-83 now states the relaxed-cell
parameters of Geilhufe et al. Tables 1, 2, which replace the planned "11.75 meV (as printed)" and Eq. (1.1); I-85: Doherty et al. Sect. 1.1
with Alkauskas et al.; I-107, I-108 confirmed and now derived; I-109: the T-center S_HR is inferred, not cited, I-292); I-261 to I-293 are the
new claims of the SEAM proposals for 7.1–7.4 and D-c (D.19–D.23), with the revision blocks' numbers (Δ₀ = −4.10 meV, t/U = 0.05–0.06,
the T-center hole binding 34.78 meV).

## Chap. 8 and Parts II–III (SPINE-II, 2026-11-01; closes LE-172)
Claim → status map read from state/SOTA.md ("R14–R16 consolidated state", "R17 final state") and LIMITS.md. Status: **REFEREED** and
**VERIFIED** (claimed and verified once) are presented as established; **CLAIMED** and **OPEN** (exact gap or not computed) are presented
as open, in the open-problems material. "Novelty" gives the state/NOVELTY.md verdict where the research claimed priority: NONE FOUND →
"to our knowledge" wording; PRIOR ART → the prior source is cited. Equation numbers marked [FT] in LIMITS were read from full text; "eq. to
fix" as in Part I. Every row is PLANNED for the derivation checker.
**SPINE-II-REV (2026-11-02).** Section numbers follow the revised spine (13.7 removed and 13.8–13.10 renumbered 13.7–13.9; 10.7 merged
into 11.7; 10.8 split into 10.7 and 10.8; 14.7 and 15.5 merged into chapter summaries; 16.8e–f moved to 19.6; the guide 17.7 → 21.7;
D.38–D.42 renumbered into section order). Status words are reconciled with the SPINE tags and SOTA "R17 final state": an estimate is
EST (presented as an estimate, open where a conclusion depends on it) even when verified; the Al bulk gap is settled (II-70);
"NONE FOUND" rows are written "derived here", never as literature consensus; the 11.8 resonator and resonant-mass rows are REFEREED
(LIMITS L-D4, L-D5), not VERIFIED; II-29 is REFEREED for the formula with its window correction VERIFIED.
**Merge of the accepted Chapter 8 and App. D.24–D.28 (2026-11-02).** II-1 to II-13 are WRITTEN with the labels of tex/ch08 and tex/appD
(D.24–D.28) as revised after the Chapter 8 review; the planning status words (REFEREED, VERIFIED, NONE FOUND, OPEN) are kept after the
label, since they say how the text presents each claim. The claims are updated from the SEAM files: II-3 cites the sources 8.2 cites
(2101.08275 enters through eq:4.2-rate and A.8); II-5 writes 𝓃_e^{(κ)} (entry 70) and attributes the single-species rule to Puff; II-7
states the theorem as written (∝ n_Z, or electron density at clamped nuclei, every q and ω ≠ 0). II-111 to II-160 are the new claims
of the SEAM proposals for 8.1–8.8 (with their revision blocks), placed after II-13 so that the Chap. 8 rows stay together.
**Merge of the accepted Chapter 10 and App. D.29–D.30 (2026-11-02).** II-15 to II-21, II-23 and II-25 to II-28 are WRITTEN with the labels of
tex/ch10 and tex/appD (D.29, D.30) as revised after the Chapter 10 review, the planning status words kept after the label. The claims

measured enhancement 1.24–1.31; II-19 carries r_med 41–76; II-25 places the ALP error in the integration step; II-28 states the
free-vertex deficit as at least 1/r. II-161 to II-183 are the new claims of the SEAM proposals for 10.1–10.10 (with their revision
blocks), placed after II-28. II-22 (11.7) and II-24 (13.5) stay with their sections.

R4); II-99 the fifth condition (e) ε_eh ≤ ε_cal and the book-ε value 0.049, worded as a bound under (a)–(d) (post-check fix 5); II-100 the
two-gain calibration chain (g_Luke, g_cal), f_ion = y_C (R2), 𝔯_coll = 1.128 [1.099, 1.152] for collection alone, above the transport
range of 20.5 and disfavored on present estimates, with the sign of 𝔯_coll − 1 open (post-check fixes 1–2; 1.144/1.154 superseded).
II-369 to II-397 are the new claims of the SEAM proposals for 19.1–19.6 and D.45, placed after II-101, with the numbers of the revised
tex and logs (65 fs, 2.3° polar, 1.9 % at 500 eV, Migdal 2.3–4.4×10⁻⁴, 298 eV, ξ_d = 0.70, y_C damage fractions, the 40 V readings
0.919/0.947, ρ_g − 1 = 0.21, R₀ from the gradient criterion); the 19.3 band is a sensitivity (II-379, R3). The machinery of the bound
lives in 19.5 (II-99 keeps its 19.4 home for Table 19.1 and points to 19.5). No claim carries novelty wording.
